### 现有测试方式
RUST UNIT TESTS (I did not execute them — instructed not to run cargo).\n\nauthoring_v2_commands.rs `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` (2003-2551), 14 tests. Two shared builders: text_document() (2029-2041) and structured_document() (2043-2075) — hand-rolled minimal JSON, not full IeltsAuthoringIRV2, used for pure apply_patch tests. temp_asset_dirs() (2017-2027) makes a nanosecond-suffixed dir under std::env::temp_dir(). Patch-vocabulary tests: replace_text_uses_character_offsets_and_marks_provenance, expression_expansion_is_bounded_and_deterministic, unsupported_node_attributes_are_rejected, structural_content_patches_support_insert_move_delete_crop_hotspot_and_cardinality, invalid_crop_and_hotspot_rects_are_rejected, replace_content_cannot_drop_an_existing_answer_slot, inserted_answer_slot_can_be_removed_only_by_an_explicit_inverse_patch, semantic_option_bank_and_answer_slot_patches_keep_registries_in_sync, undo_patch_can_restore_provenance_without_marking_user_edited. Command-level tests that need a fake app root: export_materializes_and_verifies_non_empty_assets (2343), authoring_asset_preview_resolves_only_manifest_backed_image_bytes (2376), export_rejects_non_ready_quality_state_before_materialization (2422), publish_readiness_rejects_human_unverified_stale_review_and_ai_fallback (2461), quality_refresh_rejects_stale_physical_shadow_and_preserves_resolution (2519).\n\nTHE PATTERN TO COPY for any new session/publish test (from 2376-2420 and 2461-2517): build `root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(\"ielts-authoring-v2-<name>-{suffix}\"))` with a nanos suffix; create `root.join(\"jobs\").join(job_id)`; `serde_json::from_str(include_str!(\"../../fixtures/golden/synthetic/ielts/early-approaches-authoring-v2.json\"))` as the base DS; override `authoring[\"jobId\"] = json!(job_id)` and `authoring[\"quality\"][\"state\"] = json!(\"ready\")`; write it to `job_dir.join(AUTHORING_V2_SHADOW_FILE)`; call the *_core function with `&root`; `let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root)` at the end. The fixture is the load-bearing input — it ships `audit.humanVerified: true`, `quality.state: \"review_required\"`, 1 issue and 0 hardFailures, so tests that call validate_authoring_v2_publish_readiness DIRECTLY pass (no quality refresh) while tests that go through export_authoring_v2_core get quality recomputed to review_required and assert exactly `\"authoring_v2_export_blocked:quality_state=review_required\"` (2453-2456). Two tests deliberately assert the marker and stale-review blockers: 2487-2506 writes a source-review.json with stale:true and expects `authoring_v2_export_blocked:source_review_stale`; 2509-2515 sets `authoring[\"audit\"][\"notes\"] = json!([\"AI fallback was used\"])` and expects a prefix match on `authoring_v2_export_blocked:ai_fallback=`. Any change to the marker scanner or the gate ordering must update these two.\n\nartifact_store.rs tests (643-888), 7 tests, temp_root() = temp_dir().join(format!(\"phase1-artifact-{}\", Uuid::new_v4().simple())). These are the concurrency contract and are the most valuable to keep: revisions_are_optimistic_and_recoverable (702) exercises base-mismatch rejection and pointer recovery; append_with_missing_pointer_never_overwrites_revision_one (756) asserts the exact string `\"revision_conflict:current=1:base=0\"`; concurrent_base_zero_writers_cannot_replace_each_other (794) spawns two threads on a Barrier and asserts exactly one Ok and one Err with a single surviving record; incomplete_revision_number_is_quarantined_by_monotonic_allocation (829) plants a bare revisions/1.json and asserts the next allocation is 2; artifact_relative_paths_cannot_escape_job (861) asserts \"../outside.json\", \"C:/outside.json\", \"document-ir.json\" and \"uploads/source.pdf\" are all rejected; status_is_read_only_for_a_legacy_job (871) asserts inspect_job_artifacts creates no authoring/ dir.\n\nEND-TO-END. src-tauri/src/ielts_grammar/real_pdf_acceptance.rs (#[cfg(test)] via mod.rs:23-24) holds the only full loop: run_phase5_real_pdf_edit_export (1584-1690) writes the shadow, applies phase5_answer_resolution_patches at baseRevision 0, applies one replaceText at baseRevision 1, then calls export_authoring_v2_core with the resulting revision and asserts manifest/authoring/runtime schemaVersions. Driven by `#[test] fn phase5_real_pdf_edit_and_v2_export_round_trip` (1692). It cannot run here: it needs fixtures/golden/private-real/chili-peppers.pdf, and that directory contains only README.md plus a .gitignore for *.pdf. It also was not updated by commit bb978be, which added the humanVerified/source-review/marker gates to the export path, so it needs fixing in the same change.\n\nGAPS. There is no test for get_authoring_v2_core / build_editor_session at all (no test asserts source==\"shadow\" vs \"revision\", nor the AUTHORING_V2_NOT_AVAILABLE:shadow_missing error). There is no test for the two ungated marker vectors that matter in production (requiresManualQuestionImport in authoring-ir.json, blockedAutoApplyGroups/lowConfidenceGroups in pipeline-report.json) — the only marker test uses audit.notes. There is no test that a fully resolved issue set reaches quality.state == ready, which is precisely the contradiction to fix. Frontend: no vitest/jest file covers useCanonicalEditor or actionableIssues (src/features/editor contains only the three .tsx/.ts sources).

### 意外发现
- V2 publish is currently unreachable, not merely strict. validate_authoring_v2_publish_readiness requires /audit/humanVerified == true (authoring_v2_commands.rs:255-261), but mark_user_audit forces it to false on every successful save (843) and the shadow builder hardcodes false (ielts_grammar/mod.rs:369). No patch op can reach the audit object and is_safe_node_attribute does not list humanVerified. The only document in the repo with humanVerified:true is the hand-authored golden fixture fixtures/golden/synthetic/ielts/early-approaches-authoring-v2.json, which is why the readiness unit test passes while the real pipeline cannot.
- Even without the humanVerified gate, publish would still be blocked earlier: quality.state == "ready" requires `issues.is_empty()` literally (ielts_grammar/quality.rs:187-199), and refresh_quality_report re-runs evaluate_quality on every save. The resolveIssue patch op writes details.resolution, and preserve_issue_resolutions carries it forward, but nothing removes the issue from the list — so resolving every issue still yields review_required. The publish gate's own blocker list (233-254) honours resolutions; the state check does not. Two contradictory notions of 'resolved' in one function.
- The plan's 'recursively greps historical JSON for fallback/partial strings' is directionally right but mechanically wrong, and the correction changes the fix. It is key-name driven with substring path gates, and it reads exactly two historical files. The genuinely broken rules are the three with NO path gate: requiresManualQuestionImport (a plain non-optional bool on every V1 question and group), blockedAutoApplyGroups/lowConfidenceGroups, and code == PARTIAL_RECOVERY_FAILURE. Deleting a string grep would miss all three.
- The path gates are substring tests on an accumulated dotted path, so unrelated field names open them. `lower_path.contains("ai")` is satisfied by "remaining" — pipeline-report.json.authoring.remainingReviewItems (written at auto_pipeline.rs:2657) unlocks the status/failure rules for everything beneath it. Same class of hazard for any future field containing 'ai', 'note', 'llm' or 'quality' as a substring.
- Publish-gate behaviour depends on whether cleanup has run. cleanup.rs:124-139 deletes both scanned files. Removing pipeline-report.json silently disarms the llm marker rules; removing document-ir.json flips source_review_status into its 'return the saved file verbatim' branch (source_review.rs:126-130), which can freeze stale=true forever. The same publish attempt can therefore pass or fail depending on artifact-retention settings.
- Revision cost is O(N) per keystroke-batch, not O(1). list_revision_records reads and JSON-parses every *.meta.json and is called twice per save (append_revision→recover_current_revision, then apply_authoring_v2_patches_core:164) and twice per session open. The full record vector is also returned in every session payload. At a 450ms debounce this means save latency and response size both grow linearly with edit history.
- Five of the fourteen JobArtifactPaths directories are pure dead scaffolding: assets/blobs, assets/metadata, assets/previews, preview/runtime and legacy/ are created on every ensure_job_artifact_layout call and referenced nowhere else in the crate. Plan 17.15 asks to 'migrate the asset blob logic' — there is none to migrate.
- Two unrelated directories are both called 'revisions'. authoring_commands.rs:113-121 archives V1 drafts into jobs/<id>/revisions/<timestamp>-source-replaced/, while the V2 store uses jobs/<id>/authoring/revisions/<N>.json. The publish scan reads only top-level authoring-ir.json and pipeline-report.json, so the V1 archive copies are not scanned — but the naming collision will mislead anyone writing cleanup or migration code.
- get_authoring_v2 is not read-only. recover_current_revision writes a repaired current-revision.json when the pointer lags the newest meta record (artifact_store.rs:226-237). Any plan to move the pointer into SQLite must keep both the repair and the opposite case, which hard-errors with current_revision_missing_record when the pointer is ahead.
- The session `source: "fixture"` variant in the shared TypeScript union (src/types/authoring-editor-v2.ts:15) is produced only by the browser dev fallback (devFallbackBackend.ts:2492). The Rust backend emits only "shadow" or "revision". The dev fallback's export gate is also materially softer than Rust's — it whitelists away ANSWER_KEY_MISSING_SLOT and RUNTIME_COMPILER_FAILED, spells the blocker `blocking_issues=` instead of `issues=`, and has no humanVerified, source-review or marker checks — so any publish-readiness confidence gained in dev mode is false.
- The new workspace never shows the backend quality report. src/features/editor/actionableIssues.ts derives its own six-code issue list from the current DS and ignores session.authoring.quality entirely; ExamWorkspacePage imports only deriveActionableIssues and blockerCount. So a user can clear every issue the UI shows and still be blocked by quality.state, and publishClient.ts:31-33 will explain the failure as 'missing stems, options or answers' regardless of the real cause.
- TaskGroupV2.review_state (Unreviewed/Confirmed/Edited) is written once as "unreviewed" at shadow build and is never read or updated by anything — no patch op touches it and the publish gate ignores it. Likewise SuggestedActionV2::ReplaceAsset can be emitted as a suggested action but there is no replaceAsset patch op, so that suggestion is unactionable.
- refresh_quality_report updates the document-level quality but never calls refresh_group_quality, which is private to ielts_grammar/mod.rs and runs only at shadow build (mod.rs:377). Per-task taskGroups[i].quality.score and .hardFailures therefore go stale the moment a user saves once.
- The frontend's crash-recovery snapshot is write-only: useCanonicalEditor writes it to localStorage only when a save fails and deletes it on success, and nothing ever reads the key back (only the constant declaration at line 14 matches a repo-wide grep).
- read_revision loads revisions/<N>.json with no verification against the artifact_sha256 recorded in the sibling meta file, so a hand-edited or corrupted revision is accepted as long as it still deserializes as IeltsAuthoringIRV2.

---

## DocumentIRV2 physical facts (schema/document_ir_v2.rs + pdf_facts_shadow.rs + pdf_ingest/* + docx_facts_shadow.rs + docx_ingest/*)
**数据流**：PDF (the main path). `parse_document` → authoring_commands.rs:154 `parse_document_core` → (a) `parser::parse_source_document` → `parse_pdf_with_rust_text_extractor` (parser.rs:492) → `pdf_geometry::parse_pdf_with_pdfium` builds the authoritative **DocumentIRV1** to `<job>/document-ir.json`; then (b) if `document_ir_v2_shadow_enabled()` (environment.rs:219, hardcoded `true`) → `write_pdf_facts_shadow_with_v1(job, source, upload, <job>/document-ir-v2.shadow.json, Some(&v1_ir))`. The same PDF is therefore opened and scanned TWICE by two different libraries: pdfium for V1, the pure-Rust `pdf-extract` 0.10 crate for V2.

Inside `write_pdf_facts_shadow_with_v1` (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:474): a staging dir `.pdf-shadow-txn-<uuid>/` is created with `assets/shadow/pdf/` → `extract_pdf_facts_shadow_internal` → `merge_v1_diagram_question_region_assets` → typed re-validation → `write_canonical_json_atomic` (sorted object keys) → `build_compare_report` → `commit_shadow_bundle`, which atomically installs exactly three components (artifact json, compare json, assets/shadow/pdf dir) with backup+rollback (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:714-826). A missing compare file or missing asset dir aborts the whole commit.

`extract_pdf_facts_shadow_internal` (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:838-1003), in order:
1. `load_document_with_xref_repair` (with a classic-xref/stream-Length repair pass), then `enforce_document_resource_limits` (≤1000 pages, ≤1e6 objects).
2. `collect_page_geometries` → per-page `PdfPageGeometry` (MediaBox/CropBox/Rotate/UserUnit → `pdf_to_display`).
3. `pdf_extract::output_doc(&document, &mut PdfFactsCollector)`. `OutputDev::output_character` (line 166) is called once per character with `(trm, width, spacing, font_size, char)` where `trm = Tsm × Tm × CTM` and does NOT include the font size. The collector synthesizes a parallelogram from origin, origin+advance·x̂ (advance = width·font_size) and origin+font_size·ŷ, maps all four corners through `display_point`, and emits `bbox` (axis-aligned hull), `quad` (all 8 display coords — the real rotated shape), `origin`, `baseline`, `angleRad`, `style.fontSizePt`, and a full `sourceAnchor` with bbox+nativeBBox+displayBBox+pdfToDisplay+charRange. `OutputDev::end_line` sets a transient `_sourceLineBreakAfter` on the last glyph. `stroke`/`fill` push vectorPaths with full command lists and an `isAxisAlignedRule` flag ((w≤2.6 ∧ h≥12) ∨ (h≤2.6 ∧ w≥12), line 428).
4. `page_value` (line 2336) emits the page shell with **glyphs and vectorPaths only** — spans/lines/regions/tables/assetIds/readingOrder are all literal `[]` (lines 2423-2428) — plus first-pass `quality` (classification born_digital|garbled|scanned by glyph count and unicodeErrorRatio>0.1) and a transient `_coverage`.
5. `extract_pdf_visual_assets` — embedded Image XObjects → PNG/JPEG bytes under `assets/shadow/pdf/`, asset_id `pdf-image-p{page:03}-{objnum}-{gen}`. The asset's own sourceAnchor has **no bbox**.
6. `collect_page_object_facts` (line 1894) — walks the page content stream tracking q/Q/cm, and on each `Do` computes the unit-square-through-CTM bbox → `page.imagePlacements[]` (this is where image geometry actually lives). `BMC`/`BDC` → `page.markedContent[]` with mcid/tag/ActualText/Alt; `structurePath` is always `[]` (no PDF structure-tree traversal anywhere).
7. `extract_pdf_vector_assets` — **one** `pdf-vector-p{page:03}` SVG per page, bbox = union of ALL non-rule vector paths on that page.
8. `append_page_asset_ids` + `append_page_visual_objects` → transient `_visualObjects` (kind figure|diagram) and a raster-driven re-classification of `quality` to mixed|scanned plus `requiresOcrRegions` / imageCoverageRatio.
9. `collect_pdf_annotations` → `page.annotations[]` (Widget/other, fieldName/fieldType/value/flags, plus a rendered SVG appearance fallback asset when needed).
10. `page_contains_hidden_text` per page (Tr 3/7 or ≥0.98 white fill before a text-showing op) → transient `_hiddenTextDetected`.
11. **`enrich_page` per page** — the only place the semantic-neutral layers are built: `ocr_merge::merge_provider_output` (no-op, see gaps) → `line_builder::build_lines` (baseline clustering, tolerance `(font_size.max(median_font) * 0.22).clamp(0.8, 4.5)`, vertical-overlap ≥0.35, angle delta <0.18; then intra-cluster split on gap > `max(font*2.4, typical_gap*3.5, 12.0)`) → drain nested spans into `page.spans` → `region_builder::build_regions` (occupancy-bin gutter detection with ≤1 tolerated crossing and a parallel-text requirement, full-width banner sectioning, then split a column's line run when the vertical gap exceeds 14.0 pt single-column / 10.0 pt multi-column) → `table_detector::detect_tables` → visual + annotation regions appended → `reading_order::apply_reading_order` (constraint DAG + topo sort, writes readingOrderRank/readingOrderAlternatives and OVERWRITES region.confidence with the page gutter confidence, reading_order.rs:257-265) → `ocr_router::analyze` (pairwise glyph IoU ≥0.82 → duplicateTextRatio, PDF_HIDDEN_TEXT_MISALIGNED / PDF_NATIVE_OCR_CONFLICT).
12. `coverage_ledger_from_pages` (line 2703) — one `{sourceNodeId, disposition:"unassigned", targetIds:[], reason}` entry for EVERY glyph, span, line, region, vectorPath, table, table cell, annotation, imagePlacement and markedContent node in the document, de-duplicated by id in a BTreeMap.
13. transient keys (`_coverage`, `_hiddenTextDetected`, `_visualObjects`, `_annotations`) stripped, `parser.options` assembled (preflight, physicalLayers.summary, ocrPlan), and the whole document round-tripped through `serde_json::from_value::<DocumentIRV2>` — a hard error if any field is unknown or missing.

DOCX. `open_docx` → `parse_document_model` (+ collect_drawings / collect_text_boxes / collect_composite_fallbacks / assign_numbering_labels). Then one of two paths (docx_facts_shadow.rs:302-326):
- **render-assisted** (only when `EPIC8_DOCX_RENDER_ASSIST` is truthy AND a soffice-class provider answers `--headless --version`): render to PDF, run `extract_pdf_facts_shadow` on it, keep those pages verbatim, then `bind_rendered_pages_to_ooxml` greedily matches each rendered line's whitespace-stripped text against the ordered OOXML paragraph list and `rewrite_rendered_page_anchors` stamps every glyph/span/line/region anchor with `extractionMode: "docx_rendered_fallback"` + `ooxmlPath` + a `docx_ooxml` text variant. Node ids in this path are PDF-shaped (`p001-l0001`), and tables come from the rendered PDF's geometric detector.
- **default (semantic-only)**: `build_pages` + `DocxPageBuilder`. One LINE per paragraph segment (no wrapping), glyph x advanced by `char_width` = font_size × 0.52 (ASCII) / 0.9 (non-ASCII) / 0.32 (space) / 2.5 (tab), y advanced by resolved line spacing + spacingAfter. One REGION per paragraph. Pages break only on `pageBreakBefore`, a `w:br type="page"` run, or a non-continuous section change. Tables come from OOXML with real gridWidths/rowHeights/padding/vMerge.

Persistence and read-back. The artifact is a single canonical-JSON file on disk at `<app_root>/jobs/<job_id>/document-ir-v2.shadow.json` (`util::job_dir`, util.rs:33), assets at `<app_root>/jobs/<job_id>/assets/shadow/{pdf,docx}/`, sidecars `document-ir-v2.shadow.compare.json`, `document-ir-v2.shadow.error.json`, `debug/document-ir-v2.shadow.overlay.svg`. It is **never written to SQLite** (no reference to it in db.rs, library_commands.rs, nas_package_v2.rs) and `cleanup.rs` does not delete it. Read-back is always `read_json_opt(...) -> Option<Value>` — untyped, whole-file, no size cap (util.rs:246-257) — gated by a freshness check (`physical_shadow_matches_source`, auto_pipeline.rs:249 / authoring_commands.rs:97 / `physical_shadow_matches_authoring`, authoring_v2_commands.rs:757). Three consumers exist: `ielts_grammar::build_authoring_v2_shadow` (via `semantic_lines_from_v2_shadow` + `assets`), `ielts_grammar::quality::evaluate_quality` (via `significant_physical_nodes` / `source_coverage_summary`), and the `debug_document_ir_v2_overlay` Tauri command (lib.rs:917, registered at lib.rs:1426). Since `library_items.id == job.job_id`, a recognizer given a library item id resolves its physical facts at `<app_root>/jobs/<id>/document-ir-v2.shadow.json` with no DB lookup.

### 关键签名
- `DocumentIRV2` — `#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")] #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)] pub struct DocumentIRV2 {     pub schema_version: String,     pub document_id: String,     pub job_id: String,     pub source_files: Vec<SourceFileRecordV2>,     pub pages: Vec<PageNodeV2>,     pub assets: Vec<AssetDescriptorV2>,     pub coverage_ledger: Vec<CoverageEntryV2>,     pub parser: ParserMetadataV2, }`
  - pub const DOCUMENT_IR_V2_SCHEMA_VERSION: &str = "DocumentIRV2" (line 9). Only method: `pub fn is_supported_schema_version(&self) -> bool` (line 470). document_id = format!("document-{}", &sha256[..16]) — derived from the SOURCE FILE hash, so two jobs importing the same PDF share a documentId. job_id is the per-job key and (per your verified fact) equals library_items.id.
- `PageNodeV2` — `pub struct PageNodeV2 {     pub page_index: u32,     pub width_pt: f64,     pub height_pt: f64,     pub rotation: u16,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub media_box: Option<RectV2>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub crop_box: Option<RectV2>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub page_transform: Option<PageTransformV2>,     pub glyphs: Vec<GlyphNodeV2>,     pub spans: Vec<SpanNodeV2>,     pub lines: Vec<LineNodeV2>,     pub regions: Vec<RegionNodeV2>,     pub vector_paths: Vec<VectorPathV2>,     pub tables: Vec<TableNodeV2>,     pub asset_ids: Vec<String>,     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]     pub image_placements: Vec<PdfImagePlacementV2>,     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]     pub marked_content: Vec<PdfMarkedContentV2>,     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]     pub annotations: Vec<PdfAnnotationV2>,     pub reading_order: Vec<String>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub reading_order_graph: Option<ReadingOrderGraphV2>,     pub quality: PageQualityV2, }`
  - page_index is 0-based. width_pt/height_pt are DISPLAY dims (CropBox × UserUnit, swapped for 90/270). The layer vecs are flat per-page lists; parent→child is by id list (line.span_ids → span.glyph_ids, region.child_line_ids), NOT by nesting. reading_order holds REGION ids on the PDF path (test-enforced) but mixed region+line+table ids on the synthetic DOCX path.
- `GlyphNodeV2` — `pub struct GlyphNodeV2 {     pub id: String,     pub text: String,     pub bbox: RectV2,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub quad: Option<QuadV2>,     pub origin: PointV2,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub baseline: Option<f64>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub angle_rad: Option<f64>,     pub style: TextStyleV2,     pub unicode_map_error: bool,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub hidden: Option<bool>,     pub visibility_observed: bool,     pub unicode_map_error_observed: bool,     pub geometry_basis: GlyphGeometryBasisV2,     pub confidence: f64,     pub source: GlyphSourceV2,     pub source_anchor: SourceAnchorV2, }`
  - One node PER CHARACTER (not per word). PDF id format `p{page1based:03}-g{n:05}`; DOCX `docx-p{page:03}-glyph{n:05}`. GlyphGeometryBasisV2 = PdfiumCharBox | TextMatrixDerived | OcrObserved | OoxmlLayoutDerived (line 47); PDF always emits `text_matrix_derived`, DOCX `ooxml_layout_derived` — `pdfium_char_box` and `ocr_observed` are never produced. GlyphSourceV2 = Native | Ocr (line 56); `ocr` never produced.
- `SpanNodeV2` — `pub struct SpanNodeV2 {     pub id: String,     pub glyph_ids: Vec<String>,     pub text: String,     pub bbox: RectV2,     pub style: TextStyleV2,     pub whitespace_before: WhitespaceOriginV2,     pub whitespace_after: WhitespaceOriginV2,     pub confidence: f64,     pub source_anchors: Vec<SourceAnchorV2>, }`
  - WhitespaceOriginV2 = None | Source | Synthetic (line 78). PDF span id is `{line_id}-s{n:03}` so span→line is recoverable from the id prefix; DOCX span id is `docx-p{page:03}-span{n:04}` and is NOT line-prefixed. PDF `whitespace_after` is hardcoded "none" (line_builder.rs:388) — only whitespace_before carries information. PDF span.style is copied from the FIRST glyph only (line_builder.rs:386), so a span that mixes sizes silently reports one.
- `LineNodeV2` — `pub struct LineNodeV2 {     pub id: String,     pub span_ids: Vec<String>,     pub text: String,     pub bbox: RectV2,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub baseline: Option<f64>,     pub writing_mode: WritingModeV2,     pub indentation_pt: f64,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub hanging_indent_pt: Option<f64>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub line_height_pt: Option<f64>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub hard_break_after: Option<bool>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub break_basis: Option<String>,     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]     pub inline_gaps_pt: Vec<f64>,     pub source_order: u32,     pub confidence: f64,     pub source_anchors: Vec<SourceAnchorV2>, }`
  - WritingModeV2 = HorizontalTb | VerticalRl | VerticalLr (kebab-case) — `vertical-lr` never produced. PDF id `p{page1based:03}-l{n:04}`, DOCX `docx-p{page:03}-line{n:04}`. `inline_gaps_pt` is the load-bearing field for plan §6.6/§6.7 tab-column detection: it is the ordered list of every POSITIVE inter-glyph gap in reading direction (line_builder.rs:328-339, 441) — big entries mark tab stops / column boundaries inside a single line.
- `RegionNodeV2` — `pub struct RegionNodeV2 {     pub id: String,     pub kind: PhysicalRegionKindV2,     pub bbox: RectV2,     pub child_line_ids: Vec<String>,     pub child_object_ids: Vec<String>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub column_index: Option<u32>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub section_index: Option<u32>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub z_index: Option<i32>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub reading_order_rank: Option<u32>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub reading_order_alternatives: Option<Vec<Vec<String>>>,     pub confidence: f64,     pub source_anchors: Vec<SourceAnchorV2>, }`
  - PhysicalRegionKindV2 (line 121, snake_case) = Text | Title | List | Table | Figure | Diagram | Form | Header | Footer | PageNumber | Rule | Unknown. `column_index == None` means full-width/banner, NOT column 0 (region_builder.rs:94-96). `z_index` is never set by any producer. On the PDF path only text|title|header|page_number|table|figure|diagram|form|unknown are ever emitted — `list`, `footer`, `rule` never are.
- `TableNodeV2 / TableCellV2` — `pub struct TableNodeV2 {     pub id: String,     pub bbox: RectV2,     pub rows: u32,     pub cols: u32,     pub cells: Vec<TableCellV2>,     pub detection_mode: TableDetectionModeV2,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub caption_region_id: Option<String>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub visual_fallback_asset_id: Option<String>,     pub topology_confidence: f64,     pub content_confidence: f64,     pub source_anchors: Vec<SourceAnchorV2>, }  pub struct TableCellV2 {     pub cell_id: String,     pub row: u32,     pub col: u32,     pub row_span: u32,     pub col_span: u32,     pub bbox: RectV2,     pub content_region_ids: Vec<String>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub width_pt: Option<f64>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub row_height_pt: Option<f64>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub row_height_rule: Option<String>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub vertical_alignment: Option<String>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub padding_pt: Option<TableCellPaddingV2>,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub header_scope: Option<HeaderScopeV2>,     pub border_evidence: Vec<String>,     pub confidence: f64,     pub source_anchors: Vec<SourceAnchorV2>, }`
  - TableDetectionModeV2 (line 259) = Ooxml | RulingLines | TextAlignment | VisionModel | Manual — `vision_model` and `manual` never produced. `caption_region_id` never set by any producer. `content_region_ids` always holds REGION ids (the `line_ids_for_cell` fallback name is a misnomer — table_detector.rs:35-59 maps lines back to their owning regions). width_pt/row_height_pt/row_height_rule/vertical_alignment/padding_pt are OOXML-only and are all None on the PDF path.
- `ReadingOrderGraphV2 / ReadingOrderEdgeV2` — `pub struct ReadingOrderGraphV2 {     pub primary: Vec<String>,     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]     pub alternatives: Vec<Vec<String>>,     pub edges: Vec<ReadingOrderEdgeV2>,     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]     pub cycle_edges_removed: Vec<ReadingOrderEdgeV2>,     pub confidence: f64, }  pub struct ReadingOrderEdgeV2 {     pub from: String,     pub to: String,     pub relation: String,     pub confidence: f64, }`
  - `relation` is a free String; the only four values any producer emits are "same_column_above", "column_transition", "full_width_heading_to_column", "source_line_sequence" (reading_order.rs:120, 140, 162, 188). `alternatives` holds at most ONE alternative — the pure top-to-bottom-then-left-to-right order, and only when it differs from primary (reading_order.rs:252-256). `confidence` == the page gutter confidence, in [0.55, 1.0].
- `RectV2` — `#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")] #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)] pub struct RectV2 {     pub x: f64,     pub y: f64,     pub width: f64,     pub height: f64,     pub unit: CoordinateUnitV2,     pub origin: CoordinateOriginV2,     pub page_rotation: u16,     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]     pub normalized: Option<[f64; 4]>, }`
  - x/y/width/height — NOT x0/y0/x1/y1. CoordinateUnitV2 = Pt | Emu | Px (lowercase, line 33); every producer emits `pt`. CoordinateOriginV2 = "top-left" | "bottom-left" (line 40). `normalized` = [x/W, y/H, w/W, h/H] clamped to 0..1, and ONLY glyph bboxes, vectorPath bboxes and image-placement bboxes carry it (coordinates.rs:137-155). Line, span, region and table bboxes never do (pdf_ingest/mod.rs:130-140 and docx_facts_shadow.rs:1823 both omit it).
- `TextStyleV2` — `pub struct TextStyleV2 {     pub font_name: Option<String>,     pub font_size_pt: Option<f64>,     pub weight: Option<u16>,     pub bold: Option<bool>,     pub italic: Option<bool>,     pub underline: Option<bool>,     pub strike: Option<bool>,     pub color: Option<String>,     pub background_color: Option<String>,     pub superscript: Option<bool>,     pub subscript: Option<bool>,     pub language: Option<String>, }`
  - (all fields carry #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]). On the PDF path ONLY `font_size_pt` is ever populated (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:256) — font_name, bold, italic, underline, color are permanently None. On the synthetic DOCX path all of them except background_color are populated (docx_facts_shadow.rs:1799-1813).
- `SourceAnchorV2` — `pub struct SourceAnchorV2 {     pub source_file_id: String,     pub page_index: i32,     pub node_ids: Vec<String>,     pub bbox: Option<RectV2>,     #[serde(rename = "nativeBBox")]     pub native_bbox: Option<RectV2>,     #[serde(rename = "displayBBox")]     pub display_bbox: Option<RectV2>,     pub pdf_to_display: Option<[f64; 6]>,     pub char_range: Option<SourceCharRangeV2>,     pub ooxml_path: Option<String>,     pub relationship_id: Option<String>,     pub extraction_mode: ExtractionModeV2,     pub source_hash: String,     #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]     pub variants: Vec<SourceVariantV2>, }`
  - ExtractionModeV2 (common.rs:7) = PdfNative | PdfOcr | PdfRenderedCrop | DocxOoxml | DocxRenderedFallback | Manual. `bbox` == `display_bbox` on the PDF path (both top-left); `native_bbox` is bottom-left PDF user space. `char_range` scope differs by producer: per PAGE for PDF, per PARAGRAPH for DOCX (declared in parser.options.charRangeScope, pdf_facts_shadow.rs:983 / docx_facts_shadow.rs:379).
- `AssetDescriptorV2 / DiagramQuestionRegionV2` — `pub struct AssetDescriptorV2 {     pub asset_id: String,     pub kind: AssetKindV2,     pub mime: String,     pub relative_path: String,     pub sha256: String,     pub byte_length: u64,     pub width_px: Option<u32>,     pub height_px: Option<u32>,     pub duration_ms: Option<u64>,     pub extraction_mode: AssetExtractionModeV2,     pub alt_text: Option<String>,     pub decorative: Option<bool>,     pub source_anchor: Option<SourceAnchorV2>,     pub diagram_question_region: Option<DiagramQuestionRegionV2>, }  pub struct DiagramQuestionRegionV2 {     pub question_range: [u32; 2],     pub expected_numbers: Vec<u32>,     pub recovery_status: DiagramQuestionRecoveryStatusV2,     pub number_closure: bool,     pub source_backed: bool, }`
  - AssetKindV2 (line 166) = RasterImage | VectorRender | PageCrop | Diagram | Chart | Audio | Thumbnail. AssetExtractionModeV2 (line 178) = Embedded | PageCrop | RenderedVector | DocxMedia | UserUpload. `relative_path` is job-dir-relative: "assets/shadow/pdf/<id>.<ext>" or "assets/shadow/docx/<id>.<ext>". DiagramQuestionRegionV2 is exactly the hybrid diagram+slot-overlay evidence plan §6.10 wants, and it is imported from the V1 pdfium parser, not derived in V2.

### 计划断言复核

| 判定 | 计划说 | 代码实际 | 证据 |
|---|---|---|---|
| partly-wrong | §6.1: "现有 DocumentIRV2 已经有足够丰富的物理事实" — the physical layer is rich enough, the problem is only that ielts_grammar flattens it. | Half right. Geometry (bbox/quad/baseline/angle/columns/reading-order DAG/inline gaps) is genuinely rich. But typography is nearly absent on the PDF path: TextStyleV2 gets ONLY fontSizePt; font_name, bold, italic, underline, color are permanently None because the collector emits `"style": {"fontSizePt": ...}` and nothing else. The plan's own scoring features in §6.4 ("font size / bold / spacing") and §6.5 ("字号与其他题号一致") are therefore only half-implementable on PDF today. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs:256 vs src-tauri/src/schema/common.rs:137-162; src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs:1799-1813 (DOCX populates the same struct fully)` |
| partly-wrong | §6.5: `detect_question_number_tokens(page: &DocumentPageV2, ...)` iterating `page.spans` with `parse_small_numeric_token`. | The type is `PageNodeV2`, not `DocumentPageV2`, and there is no `DocumentPageV2` anywhere in the repo. `page.spans` is a flat per-page Vec whose parent line is recoverable only from the id prefix (`{line_id}-s{nnn}`) on the PDF path — and NOT recoverable at all on the synthetic DOCX path, where span ids are `docx-p{page:03}-span{n:04}` with no line prefix. Spans are also word-like, not token-like: a span breaks only on a source space glyph or a gap > `max(median_font*0.28, median_gap*1.75, 1.0)`, so "5" in "5 Which..." is a separate span but "(a)" or "27." is not necessarily split from what follows. | `src-tauri/src/schema/document_ir_v2.rs:295; src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/line_builder.rs:348, 380, 407-421; src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs:800-804` |
| partly-wrong | §6.5: "-0.30 位于页脚/页码区域" — header/footer/page-number exclusion can key off region role. | `region_kind` can only produce header\|page_number under absurdly narrow conditions: "header" requires bbox.y < 50.0 AND the text to contain the literal "ielts"; "page_number" requires bbox.y > 0.86·pageHeight AND the text to be digits/whitespace only. `PhysicalRegionKindV2::Footer` and `::Rule` are never emitted by any producer. Real running heads/feet land in kind="text" and are counted as significant source nodes. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/region_builder.rs:146-162; src-tauri/src/ielts_grammar/quality.rs:3443 (the header\|footer\|page_number\|rule skip that consequently almost never fires)` |
| wrong | §6.10 表格: "优先使用 DocumentIRV2.pages[].tables[]" with a `PhysicalTableV2` type and `table.rows.map(...)`. | The type is `TableNodeV2`; `rows` is a `u32` count, not an iterable — cells are a flat `Vec<TableCellV2>` addressed by (row, col). More importantly the PDF detector emits AT MOST ONE table per page: `detect_tables` tries the ruled candidate, and only `if tables.is_empty()` tries the borderless one, and neither loops. Worse, the ruled detector clusters ALL rule x/y coordinates on the page into ONE grid, so two separate tables on one page collapse into a single spurious grid (and bail out entirely if xs·ys > 400). The borderless detector additionally refuses to run if the page has ANY vector path at all. | `src-tauri/src/schema/document_ir_v2.rs:241-255; src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/table_detector.rs:472-488, 347-357, 396-402` |
| wrong | §6.10 表格 / §17.5: "Word 原生 table 优先产生 physical table". | Inverted whenever render assist is on. `extract_docx_facts_shadow_with_renderer` uses the rendered PDF's pages *instead of* `build_pages`, so the OOXML tables — the only ones with real gridWidths, rowHeights, padding, verticalAlignment and vMerge-derived rowSpan — are discarded and replaced by the PDF geometric detector's single-table-per-page guess. `detectionMode: "ooxml"` can only appear on the semantic-only path. | `src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs:302-326 (rendered_pages wins over build_pages) vs :955-1105 (add_table, the only producer of detectionMode ooxml + widthPt/rowHeightPt/paddingPt)` |
| partly-wrong | §6.10 diagram: source-crop + normalized hotspot fallback needs to be built. | The evidence half already exists and is typed: `DiagramQuestionRegionV2 { question_range, expected_numbers, recovery_status, number_closure, source_backed }` on a `kind: "page_crop"` asset with display+native bboxes. But it is *imported from the V1 pdfium parser*, not derived in V2 — and it is merged AFTER enrich_page, so the crop is added to `page.assetIds` and `document.assets` but NEVER becomes a region. A recognizer walking `page.regions` cannot see it. | `src-tauri/src/schema/common.rs:213-229; src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs:518-660 (called at :551, after extract_pdf_facts_shadow_internal returns)` |
| confirmed | §6.2/§6.3: a Question Layout Graph intermediate layer must be added. | Confirmed, and nothing pre-exists: no `QuestionLayoutGraph`, `question_blocks`, `LayoutGraph`, `recognize_local` or `LocalRecognitionOutcome` symbol appears anywhere in src-tauri. ielts_grammar/ has 16 files and none of them is a graph builder. | `repo-wide grep over src-tauri/src for QuestionLayoutGraph\|question_layout\|LayoutGraph\|recognize_local\|LocalRecognitionOutcome returns zero hits; ls src-tauri/src/ielts_grammar/` |
| confirmed | §6.3: reading order should be recovered before task classification, i.e. downstream should consume a reading order. | Confirmed and worse than stated: reading order IS computed well (a real constraint DAG with 4 relation types, cycle removal, primary + alternative, per-page confidence) and then completely ignored. `semantic_lines_from_v2_shadow` iterates `page.lines` in array order — which `build_lines` sorted by raw (min y, min x) across the WHOLE page, i.e. it interleaves columns. Multi-column question papers are already scrambled before any grammar rule runs. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/reading_order.rs:86-283 produces it; src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/line_builder.rs:448-470 sorts lines page-globally by y-then-x; src-tauri/src/ielts_grammar/instruction_zone.rs:176-181 iterates page.lines and never reads readingOrder` |
| partly-wrong | §17.4 ocr_router/ocr_merge: "保留 selective OCR 决策" / "保留 native/OCR 冲突；把冲突作为 field evidence". | There is no OCR at all. `ocr_merge::merge_provider_output` keys on a transient `page["_ocrProviderOutput"]` that NO producer in the repo ever writes (grep finds it only inside ocr_merge.rs itself and its tests), so it returns `OcrMergeResult::default()` on every real page. `build_ocr_plan_summary` self-declares `"engine": "not_configured"`. `local_ocr_enabled()` defaults false. GlyphSourceV2::Ocr, GlyphGeometryBasisV2::OcrObserved, ExtractionModeV2::PdfOcr and SourceAnchorV2.variants are unreachable on the PDF path. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/ocr_merge.rs:94-100; repo-wide grep for _ocrProviderOutput; src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/mod.rs:645; src-tauri/src/environment.rs:215` |
| already-done | §17.4 line_builder: "输出明确 lineId/spanId/glyphId，禁止提前压平重要 gap". | Already done, and the gap preservation is real: `inlineGapsPt` records every positive inter-glyph gap, `hardBreakAfter`/`breakBasis: "pdf_output_device_end_line"` are set only when the output device actually reported end_line, and there is a regression test asserting breaks are not fabricated. Ids are explicit and hierarchical for PDF. The remaining gap is DOCX span ids not being line-prefixed. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/line_builder.rs:504, 510-518, and test `unobserved_line_breaks_are_not_fabricated_and_gaps_are_recorded` at :571` |
| partly-wrong | §17.4 reading_order: "输出 primary + alternatives；低置信度时不给下游伪确定顺序". | primary + alternatives + edges + cycleEdgesRemoved + confidence are all already emitted, and `PAGE_READING_ORDER_AMBIGUOUS` is pushed when columnCount>1 ∧ gutterConfidence<0.72. But the "don't hand downstream a false certainty" half is violated: `page.readingOrder` is unconditionally set to `graph.primary`, and every region in a multi-column page gets the SAME full page-level alternatives array copied onto it. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/mod.rs:318-336, 366-371; src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/reading_order.rs:260-262` |
| confirmed | §17.3: "统一与 pdf_geometry.rs 的 PDFium/文本提取入口，避免同一 PDF 多次扫描". | Confirmed, and the two scanners are not interchangeable. pdf_geometry uses pdfium but collects only `(unicode_char, origin_x, origin_y)` per char — no font size, no font name, no char bbox — so V1 line bboxes are inferred from char origins. pdf_facts_shadow uses the `pdf-extract` crate and gets font_size + advance width + full text matrix. Unifying on pdfium would need `loose_bounds()`/`scaled_font_size()`/`font_name()` wired in; unifying on pdf-extract would lose the diagram page-crop rendering that only pdfium can do. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_geometry.rs:121-125 (CharWithOrigin), :351-372 (collect_chars_with_origin), and zero font-related identifiers in the file; src-tauri/src/parser.rs:502; src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs:166-267` |
| partly-wrong | §17.3: "将 shadow artifact 写入逻辑与 facts collector 分离" and "只做 PDF 物理事实，不推断 IELTS". | The second half already holds — pdf_facts_shadow and pdf_ingest contain no IELTS vocabulary except one incidental "ielts"/"reading passage" string test in region_kind. The first half does not: `write_pdf_facts_shadow_with_v1` mixes the 3-component staging/backup/rollback transaction, the V1 diagram-asset bridge and the compare-report write into one 60-line closure, and `extract_pdf_facts_shadow_internal` both collects facts and writes asset bytes (via the `asset_root: Option<&Path>` parameter that silently degrades to "no assets persisted" when None). | `src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs:474-514, 838-1003, 1466-1471; src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/region_builder.rs:150-152` |
| partly-wrong | §17.5: "与 PDF 输出同一 DocumentIRV2". | Same schema, materially different semantics. DOCX (default path) has: no vectorPaths, no imagePlacements, no markedContent, no annotations, no pageTransform, no mediaBox/cropBox, no readingOrderGraph, no `normalized` on any bbox, `hardBreakAfter: true` on every line, `readingOrder` containing region AND line AND table ids interleaved, one line per paragraph regardless of wrapping, and glyph x positions from a 0.52·fontSize monospace-ish estimate. Also: DOCX never gets a V2 artifact at all on the auto-import path, which filters `source.file_type == "pdf"`. | `src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs:1106-1145 (finish), :919-921, :944-945, :1111-1114, :1784-1797; src-tauri/src/auto_pipeline.rs:1487` |
| partly-wrong | §17.5: "DrawingML/SmartArt/浮动文本框难以结构化时生成 visual fallback asset + contained text regions". | Floating text boxes DO become regions (positioned by EMU x/y when both are present, docx_facts_shadow.rs:1690-1700). SmartArt and Chart do NOT: `collect_composite_fallbacks` extracts their accessible text into `DocxCompositeDrawing.text`, and that text is published only inside `parser.options.ooxml.compositeDrawings[].accessibleText`. It never becomes a glyph, span, line, region or asset, so a geometry-first recognizer sees nothing for SmartArt content. | `src-tauri/src/docx_ingest/smartart.rs:7-39; src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs:573-581, :1690-1700` |
| partly-wrong | §18 P4-T01: "DocumentIRV2 producer schema gate". | Partly there and partly missing. The Rust gate is strong: every producer round-trips through `serde_json::from_value::<DocumentIRV2>` and every struct is `deny_unknown_fields`, so an unknown key fails the whole extraction. But `contracts/document-ir-v2.schema.json` (18 KB, JSON-Schema 2020-12) is referenced by NOTHING — no Rust, no TS, no script — so the Rust structs and the published contract can drift silently. | `src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs:998-1003 and src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs:403-408 (the Rust gate); repo-wide grep for "document-ir-v2.schema.json" outside contracts/ returns zero hits` |

### 改动点

| 风险 | 文件::符号 | 改什么 | 前置 |
|---|---|---|---|
| medium | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/table_detector.rs::detect_tables (line 464), ruled_candidate (line 303), borderless_candidate (line 387), make_table (line 200)` | Highest-value single fix for plan §6.10 and §17.4. (1) Return MULTIPLE tables per page: replace the `if tables.is_empty()` gate at :476 with connected-component clustering of rules before gridding, so two tables on one page do not merge into one grid (:347-357 currently clusters every rule x and y on the page into a single xs/ys pair). (2) Let the borderless detector run on pages that have vector paths — the `if page.vectorPaths is non-empty { return None }` guard at :396-402 means any single underline or header rule anywhere on the page kills borderless detection entirely, which is exactly the IELTS completion-table case. (3) Make table and cell ids page-unique: `format!("p-table-{:04}", index + 1)` at :291 and `format!("p-table-{}-r{}-c{}", ...)` at :276 produce "p-table-0001" / "p-table-1-r0-c0" on EVERY page, so ids collide document-wide; the coverage ledger's BTreeMap silently de-duplicates them (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:2761) and hides the collision. (4) `headerScope` is `if row == 0 {"row"} else {"none"}` at :283 — replace with real header inference or emit None rather than a guess. | — |
| medium | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs::PdfFactsCollector::output_character (line 166), specifically the style literal at line 256` | Two separate problems in one line. (a) `"style": {"fontSizePt": clean_number(safe_font_size * page.geometry.user_unit)}` uses the raw Tf operand and applies only UserUnit — it does NOT apply the CTM scale, while the bbox on the very next lines DOES (all four corners go through `trm` then `display_point`). So under a scaling CTM, `style.fontSizePt` and `bbox.height` disagree. pdf-extract's own reference output computes `sqrt(\|trm.transform_vector(font_size, font_size)\|)` (crate lib.rs:2160-2162). Until fixed, any geometry-first scorer should derive effective size from `bbox.height` or the `quad`, not from `style.fontSizePt`. (b) Nothing but fontSizePt is emitted, so fontName/bold/italic/color are unavailable on the PDF path — pdf-extract's OutputDev signature does not expose the font object, so populating them requires either a fork/upgrade of pdf-extract or a parallel pdfium pass (see pdf_geometry unification below). | — |
| medium | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/mod.rs::enrich_page (line 270)` | The single insertion seam for the new geometry-first pass, and the place to stop discarding indexes. `RegionBuild.line_to_region` (region_builder.rs:9) is the line→region map the recognizer needs and it is dropped after apply_reading_order — either persist it or return it from enrich_page. Also add a table→region and region→column index. Note the fixed pipeline order at :275-311: tables are detected AFTER regions and then RETRO-LABEL overlapping regions to kind="table" (table_detector.rs:508-533), so a region's kind depends on detection order; visual and annotation regions are appended after that and carry no columnIndex/sectionIndex, which makes them full-width in `base_compare`. | — |
| medium | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/region_builder.rs::region_kind (line 114), build_regions paragraph split at line 307-309, detect_gutters (line 29)` | Plan §6.4 needs region roles this function cannot supply. `region_kind` can only return title\|header\|page_number\|text; "header" requires y<50 AND the literal "ielts" in the text, "page_number" requires y>0.86·H AND digits-only. Add repeated-position header/footer detection ACROSS pages (nothing in the codebase compares pages to each other) and emit `Footer`/`List`/`Rule`. Separately, the region split threshold is an absolute `gap > 14.0` (single-column) / `gap > 10.0` (multi-column) in points, not font-relative — a 9 pt body font and a 14 pt body font get the same paragraph boundary, which will over- or under-split. `detect_gutters` bins at pageWidth/4 clamped to 32..512, tolerates ≤1 crossing, and requires a gap ≥ 3.5% of page width with parallel text on both sides — reasonable, but returns a confidence of only `(0.55 + meanGapRatio).clamp(0.55, 1.0)`, so a perfect 2-column page reports ~0.60 and trips the `<0.72` PAGE_READING_ORDER_AMBIGUOUS warning at pdf_ingest/mod.rs:366. Recalibrate before you gate anything on region confidence. | — |
| low | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/reading_order.rs::apply_reading_order (line 86), specifically lines 257-265` | `object.insert("confidence", json!(clean(gutter_confidence)))` OVERWRITES each region's own confidence (0.86 for a columned region, 0.94 for full-width, set in region_builder.rs:200) with the page-level gutter confidence. Any recognizer that reads `region.confidence` expecting a region-quality signal gets a page-layout signal instead. Also `readingOrderAlternatives` copies the entire page-level alternatives array onto EVERY region in a multi-column page (:261) — pure duplication that multiplies artifact size by the region count. Move both to the page-level ReadingOrderGraphV2 and leave region.confidence alone. | — |
| medium | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/line_builder.rs::build_lines (line 445), build_spans (line 306), lines 502-507` | (1) `indentationPt` is the ABSOLUTE `bbox.x` (:502), not an indent relative to the column's left edge, so it is useless for §6.6's `same_column_or_hanging_indent` / §6.7's `hanging_indent_matches` until you subtract the region's or column's left edge. (2) `lineHeightPt` is `bbox.height` (:503) — the glyph box height, i.e. roughly the font size — NOT the inter-baseline distance, so it cannot be used for §6.6's `vertical_gap_below_threshold`; compute leading from consecutive `baseline` deltas instead. (3) `hangingIndentPt` is NEVER set on the PDF path (only DOCX sets it, from `w:ind/@hanging`); the geometric equivalent has to be derived. (4) `sourceAnchors` at :507 pushes ONE anchor PER GLYPH, each carrying bbox + nativeBBox + displayBBox + a 6-number pdfToDisplay + charRange + a 64-char sourceHash. Region anchors then re-concatenate all of them (mod.rs:150-169), and table/cell anchors concatenate them a third and fourth time (table_detector.rs:62-74, 156-198). Collapsing a line's anchors to one merged anchor with a merged nodeIds list is the single largest artifact-size win available. (5) The page-global line sort at :448-470 is (min y, min x) across the whole page, which interleaves columns before regions are built. | — |
| low | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs::coverage_ledger_from_pages (line 2703)` | Emits an "unassigned" ledger entry for EVERY node at EVERY granularity — glyphs, spans, lines, regions, vectorPaths, tables, table cells, annotations, imagePlacements, markedContent. For a per-character glyph layer this is the dominant cost in the file and it carries no information a `page.glyphs[].id` scan could not derive. ### 现有测试方式
Everything is `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` in-crate; there is no tests/ directory for this subsystem. Run with `cargo test -p <crate> <name>` from src-tauri/ (I did not run anything — instructed not to).

Unit tests to copy for a new geometry pass, all small and self-contained:
- `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/line_builder.rs:553 rotated_glyphs_are_retained_as_vertical_lines` and `:571 unobserved_line_breaks_are_not_fabricated_and_gaps_are_recorded` — the pattern is a `glyph(id, text, x, y, angle) -> Value` helper (:533) feeding a `json!({"pageIndex","rotation","glyphs":[...]})` page straight into `build_lines`. This is the cheapest way to test any new line/token logic; note the fixture omits `unit` in the bbox, which works because build_lines reads raw Values and never goes through RectV2.
- `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/reading_order.rs:312 graph_uses_line_membership_and_column_constraints` — `region(id, x, y, column)` helper (:299) + a `line_to_region` BTreeMap, asserts exact primary order AND that all four relation strings appear.
- `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/table_detector.rs:565 missing_internal_rule_creates_a_merged_cell_with_cell_specific_evidence` — `rule(id, start, end)` helper (:541) building vectorPath Values, calls `make_table` directly; asserts colSpan merging plus non-empty borderEvidence and per-cell sourceAnchors.
- `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/coordinates.rs:285 rotation_matrices_map_crop_box_to_upright_display` — loops rotation ∈ {0,90,180,270} with UserUnit 2.0 and asserts display dims and that the CropBox maps to (0,0). Extend this for any coordinate change; it is the only rotation test.
- `src-tauri/src/pdf_ingest/ocr_merge.rs:211/:228/:258` — three merge tests, the only coverage of the (dead) OCR path.

End-to-end PDF tests in `src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs` (mod tests starts ~:2900):
- `:2984 extracts_native_glyph_boxes_font_size_angle_and_zero_based_anchors` — the baseline shape assertion, uses `fixtures/parser/complex-reading.pdf`.
- `:3120 all_synthetic_pdf_fixtures_round_trip_physical_layers_without_schema_loss` — THE invariant test to extend. Iterates 15 synthetic fixtures in `fixtures/golden/synthetic/pdf/` (pdf-two-column, pdf-three-column, pdf-ruled-table, pdf-borderless-table, pdf-flowchart, pdf-vector-diagram, pdf-map-hotspot, pdf-header-footer, pdf-hidden-ocr, pdf-image-only, pdf-mixed-text-image, pdf-native-ocr-conflict, pdf-broken-font, pdf-rotated-page, pdf-question-before-passage) and asserts: typed DocumentIRV2 parse, glyphs ⇒ non-empty lines AND spans, physicalLayers.summary.lineCount == sum of page line counts, and `set(page.readingOrder) == set(region ids)` — that last one is the contract that makes PDF readingOrder region-only.
- `:3196 available_private_real_pdf_corpus_preserves_physical_layer_invariants` — driven by `fixtures/golden/manifest.json`, skips when the private corpus is absent.
- `:3288 assert_column_fixture` / `:3317 synthetic_two_and_three_column_reading_order_is_geometry_aware` — the column-count harness to extend for §6.4.
- `:3323 ruled_and_borderless_table_candidates_are_shadow_only_and_anchored`, `:3361 hidden_ocr_conflicts_are_reported_without_native_text_overwrite`, `:3383 embedded_images_are_written_to_shadow_asset_store_with_integrity_metadata`, `:3439 indirect_page_resources_preserve_real_chili_passage_and_answer_images`, `:3480 real_organisational_image_only_answer_pages_route_to_full_page_ocr_plan`, `:3544 mixed_native_text_and_raster_routes_only_image_regions_without_performing_ocr`, `:3576 vector_figure_fallback_and_widget_regions_are_shadow_only_and_anchored`.
- Transaction tests: `:3727 shadow_bundle_commit_replaces_artifacts_and_assets_as_one_staged_set`, `:3788 ..._mid_commit_failure_restores_previous_bytes`, `:3846 ..._rollback_failure_is_fail_closed_and_preserves_backup_root`, `:3916 pdf_preflight_limits_fail_with_stable_codes`, `:3961 repaired_classic_structure_has_canonical_xref_and_stream_length`. Helpers: `fixture_source()` (:2917), `temporary_pdf_source()` (:2955), `widget_pdf_bytes()` (:2923).

DOCX tests in `src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs` (mod tests ~:2150): `:2239 rich_docx_shadow_preserves_structure_assets_and_strict_schema`, `:2437 paragraph_line_and_page_breaks_create_distinct_segments_without_repeating_numbering`, `:2479 external_image_sets_publish_block_and_explicit_issue_code`, `:2512 checked_in_phase3_docx_packages_reach_typed_shadow_ir` (docx-external-image / -floating-text-box / -section-columns / -smartart / -table-merged-cells), `:2630 adversarial_phase3_docx_fixtures_enforce_the_checked_in_matrix`, plus the docx commit/rollback trio at :2288/:2315/:2370. The one to copy for any render-path work is **`:2521 render_provider_pdf_geometry_binds_split_option_columns_to_ooxml`**: it injects a closure renderer returning a checked-in `render-assisted-two-column-options.provider-output.pdf` into `extract_docx_facts_shadow_with_renderer`, then asserts `regions[].columnIndex == {0,1}`, that each option line's owning region is in readingOrder, and that anchors carry `extractionMode == "docx_rendered_fallback"` with a non-null `ooxmlPath`. Helpers: `package_bytes()` (:2177), `job_and_source()` (:2208), `phase3_fixture_root()`, `assert_render_binding()` (:2831). OOXML table topology has its own test at `src-tauri/src/docx_ingest/tables.rs:278-298` (gridSpan 2 / rowSpan 2 on the anchor cell, rowSpan 0 on the vMerge continuation).

Schema-level round-trip lives in `src-tauri/src/schema/mod.rs:86 document_ir_v2_round_trip_preserves_schema_version_and_empty_physical_layers` — the minimal valid DocumentIRV2 literal (mod.rs:88-135) is the fastest fixture to clone when adding fields, and `:210 canonical_json_is_deterministic_and_keeps_array_order` guards the on-disk encoding.

Full-chain acceptance: `src-tauri/src/ielts_grammar/real_pdf_acceptance.rs` — `:1894 chili_real_pdf_reaches_all_declared_acceptance_truth`, `:1921 petri_real_pdf_reaches_all_declared_acceptance_truth`, plus policy unit tests at `:1788`, `:1820`, `:1860`. Spec files: `fixtures/golden/phase4-eight-pdf-acceptance.json`, `fixtures/golden/manifest.json`; reports to `tmp/phase4-real-pdf-acceptance/report.json` and `tmp/phase5-real-pdf-acceptance/report.json`.

Not covered anywhere, and worth adding before you change them: `region_builder::build_regions` has NO tests at all (no `#[cfg(test)]` block in the file), `ocr_router::analyze` has none, and `coverage_ledger_from_pages` has none.

### 意外发现
- A PDF glyph bbox is NOT a real glyph box. `geometry_basis` is always `text_matrix_derived`: the collector synthesizes a parallelogram from the text-matrix origin, an advance vector of length `width × font_size`, and a height vector of length `font_size` (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:183-196). Consequences a geometry-first recognizer must internalize: for unrotated text `glyph.baseline == glyph.bbox.y + glyph.bbox.height` EXACTLY, descenders fall entirely outside the box, there is no cap-height or x-height information, and `bbox.height ≈ font_size` rather than the inked extent. `quad` (8 display coords) is the only faithful shape for rotated text; `bbox` is its axis-aligned hull.
- Table and cell ids collide across pages. `format!("p-table-{:04}", index + 1)` where index is always 0 (one table per page max) yields the literal id "p-table-0001" on every page of the document, and cells are "p-table-1-r0-c0" everywhere (table_detector.rs:291, :276). Every other node id is page-prefixed. The coverage ledger's BTreeMap-by-id de-duplication (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:2761) silently swallows the collision, so it does not surface as an error — it surfaces as missing tables. Any global id→node index built by the new recognizer will be wrong on multi-page PDFs.
- OCR does not exist in this pipeline. `ocr_merge::merge_provider_output` is keyed on a transient `page["_ocrProviderOutput"]` that no code in the repo ever writes (grep finds it only inside ocr_merge.rs and its own tests), so it returns default on every real page. `build_ocr_plan_summary` self-declares `"engine": "not_configured"` and `local_ocr_enabled()` defaults false. Four schema variants — GlyphSourceV2::Ocr, GlyphGeometryBasisV2::OcrObserved, ExtractionModeV2::PdfOcr, and SourceAnchorV2.variants on the PDF path — are unreachable. The `requiresOcrRegions` / classification=scanned machinery is a plan, not a result.
- DOCX geometry is estimated, and it is the DEFAULT. `EPIC8_DOCX_RENDER_ASSIST` is off unless explicitly set (docx_ingest/render_fallback.rs:103-112), so real DOCX imports go through DocxPageBuilder, which advances x by `font_size × 0.52` per ASCII char, emits one line per paragraph with no wrapping, and lets glyph x run past the content box (only the line bbox is clamped, docx_facts_shadow.rs:1097). Nothing in the artifact marks these coordinates as synthetic except `parser.options.renderAssist.geometryAuthority == "ooxml-semantic-only"` and `glyph.geometryBasis == "ooxml_layout_derived"`.
- `page.readingOrder` has two incompatible shapes. PDF: region ids only, asserted by test (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:3175-3186 `assert_eq!(order_ids, region_ids)`). Synthetic DOCX: region id AND line id pushed per paragraph (docx_facts_shadow.rs:944-945) plus table ids (:1111-1114), all interleaved. The schema is just `Vec<String>` so both validate.
- Region `confidence` is not about the region. `apply_reading_order` overwrites whatever region_builder computed (0.86 columned / 0.94 full-width) with the page-level gutter confidence for every region (reading_order.rs:263). And because gutter confidence is `(0.55 + meanGapRatio).clamp(0.55, 1.0)`, a clean two-column page scores around 0.60 and trips the `<0.72` PAGE_READING_ORDER_AMBIGUOUS warning — the metric is miscalibrated for its own threshold.
- `style.fontSizePt` and `bbox` can disagree. fontSizePt is the raw Tf operand × UserUnit (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:256) and never picks up the CTM scale, while the bbox corners are all pushed through `trm` (which includes CTM) and then `pdf_to_display`. pdf-extract's own reference consumer computes effective size as `sqrt(|trm.transform_vector(font_size, font_size)|)` (crate lib.rs:2160-2162). Under a scaling CTM the reported font size is wrong while the geometry is right — so font-size-equality scoring (plan §6.5) should use bbox height, not style.fontSizePt.
- `normalized` is present on exactly three node kinds. Only glyph bboxes, vectorPath bboxes and image-placement bboxes carry `normalized: [x/W, y/H, w/W, h/H]`, because only `PdfPageGeometry::display_rect` emits it (coordinates.rs:137-155). Lines, spans, regions and tables go through `pdf_ingest::mod::rect` (:130-140) or `docx_facts_shadow::rect` (:1823), which both omit it. Plan §6.10's `normalizedRect` hotspots must be computed from `page.widthPt`/`heightPt` (or `pageTransform.displayToNormalized`), not read off the region.
- The V1 pdfium parser — nominally "authoritative" per its own comment at parser.rs:499 — throws away more than the V2 shadow keeps. `collect_chars_with_origin` (pdf_geometry.rs:351) takes only `(unicode_char, origin_x, origin_y)`: no font size, no font name, no char bbox. There is not a single font-related identifier in the whole 175 KB file. So the two PDF readers are not redundant — pdf-extract has the typography, pdfium has the rendering (page crops) — and neither alone can produce a font-aware DocumentIRV2.
- SmartArt and Chart text is invisible to any layer-based recognizer. `collect_composite_fallbacks` (docx_ingest/smartart.rs:7) harvests `<a:t>`/`<v>` text from diagramData/chart parts into `DocxCompositeDrawing.text`, and it is published ONLY at `parser.options.ooxml.compositeDrawings[].accessibleText` (docx_facts_shadow.rs:573-581). It never becomes a glyph, span, line, region or asset. `ParserMetadataV2.options` being a free `BTreeMap<String, Value>` is what makes this side channel possible — it is also the escape hatch for adding new producer metadata without touching the deny_unknown_fields structs.
- `deny_unknown_fields` on every struct turns a typo into silent data loss, not a compile error. Producers build untyped `Value` and are validated once at the very end; a failure writes `document-ir-v2.shadow.error.json` and is then swallowed by both call sites (authoring_commands.rs:216-227, auto_pipeline.rs:1509-1519), so the job proceeds with zero physical facts and no user-visible error. Add a field to a `json!` literal without adding it to the Rust struct and the whole document disappears.
- One vector asset per page, bbox = union of ALL non-rule paths on that page (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:1601-1680, asset_id `pdf-vector-p{page:03}`). Two separate diagrams on one page produce one merged asset with a bbox spanning both. Same collapse as the table detector, different code path.
- Source anchors are duplicated 3-5× per character. A glyph's anchor (bbox + nativeBBox + displayBBox + 6-number pdfToDisplay + charRange + 64-char sourceHash) appears in glyph.sourceAnchor, again once-per-glyph in its line's `sourceAnchors` (line_builder.rs:507), again in its region's `sourceAnchors` (pdf_ingest/mod.rs:150-169), and again in the overlapping table's and each cell's anchors (table_detector.rs:62-74, :156-198). The coverage ledger then adds a separate entry per glyph, span, line, region, cell and path. I could not measure the resulting file — no generated shadow artifact exists in the working tree and I did not run cargo — but the duplication factor is verified from the code, and `read_json` loads the whole file into a String and then a full untyped Value DOM with no size cap (util.rs:246).
- `contracts/document-ir-v2.schema.json` (18 KB, JSON-Schema 2020-12, listed in contracts/contract-manifest.json) is referenced by no Rust, TypeScript, or script file in the repo. It can drift from the Rust structs indefinitely without any test noticing — which is precisely the "DocumentIRV2 producer schema gate" that plan P4-T01 asks for.
- `document_id` is derived from the SOURCE FILE sha256 (`format!("document-{}", &sha256[..16])`, pdf_facts_shadow.rs:975), not from the job. Two jobs importing the same PDF get the same documentId with different jobIds — and `physical_shadow_matches_authoring` (authoring_v2_commands.rs:757) matches on documentId AND jobId, so this is currently benign, but it means documentId is not a usable primary key for per-job recognition output.

---

## V1 authoring pipeline and parser (src-tauri/src/authoring_pipeline.rs + parser.rs), and the V1→V2 bridge that currently makes every "V2" artifact a derivative of V1
**数据流**：Today, for a new import (verified end to end):

1. Frontend stage A: `createImportJob` + `importSourceFile` (useImportFiles.ts:126-127) writes job.json and uploads/<stored_name>. `job_store::save_job` immediately mirrors into SQLite (see step 9).
2. Frontend stage B: `runAutoPipeline(jobId, {executionMode:"localOnly", target:"editableDraft"})` (useImportFiles.ts:149) → Tauri `run_auto_pipeline` (lib.rs:1292) → `auto_pipeline::run_auto_pipeline_core_with_gateway` (auto_pipeline.rs:1381), inside `spawn_blocking`.
3. Guard: `if dir.join("authoring-ir.json").exists() && !allow_overwrite { return Err("editable_draft_exists...") }` (auto_pipeline.rs:1402). The re-entry guard is keyed on the V1 artifact.
4. Parse: `parser::parse_source_document` (auto_pipeline.rs:1449) → for PDF, `pdf_geometry::parse_pdf_with_pdfium` (pdf_geometry.rs:177) using pdfium-render, real per-glyph geometry, blocks built by `document_block_with_bbox` (pdf_geometry.rs:2304) whose blockType/roleHint/html come from parser::*_pub. Result = DocumentIRV1, written to `document-ir.json` (auto_pipeline.rs:1464).
5. Second, independent scan: `write_pdf_facts_shadow_with_v1` (auto_pipeline.rs:1496) using pdf_extract + lopdf → `document-ir-v2.shadow.json`. This is a *separate* engine reading the same PDF; it is guarded by `if current_physical_shadow(&dir, &job).is_none()` and PDF-only (DOCX gets no V2 shadow on the auto path — only `parse_document_core` does DOCX, authoring_commands.rs:203).
6. Recognize: `make_dynamic_split_candidates` → `merge_answer_source_candidates` → `split-candidates.json` (auto_pipeline.rs:1675-1678); then `make_dynamic_authoring_ir` → `authoring-ir.json` (1684-1685). All structure decisions — passage range, question groups, kinds, option banks, answer slots — happen here, from DocumentIRV1 only.
7. Optional cloud diagnostics (vision transcription, outline compare) mutate `ir` but explicitly never replace it (auto_pipeline.rs:1619-1623, 2404-2415).
8. Derive V2: `write_pipeline_authoring_v2_shadow` (auto_pipeline.rs:2027) → `ielts_grammar::build_authoring_v2_shadow(job, &ir, &split, doc, physical_shadow)`. The group loop is over `split["questionGroupCandidates"]`; `physical_shadow` only upgrades line geometry. Output `authoring-ir-v2.shadow.json` + `.compare.json`. `evaluate_quality` runs inside (ielts_grammar/mod.rs:375) and includes the blocking V1-compatibility probe.
9. Every `update_job` in that flow calls `job_store::save_job` → `library_commands::upsert_reading_job` → payload = the whole authoring-ir.json → `upsert_exam` + `upsert_library_item_from_exam(..., "ReadingAuthoringIRV1")` (library_commands.rs:106). So library_items.revision_payload_json is a V1 blob and a new revision row is appended on each status transition.
10. Cleanup: `minimize_process_artifacts_after_authoring` (auto_pipeline.rs:2186) first calls `write_authoring_project` — which HARD-reads authoring-ir.json (cleanup.rs:44) — then deletes document-ir.json, split-candidates.json, pipeline-report.json, cache/, preview/. Both V2 shadows survive.

Then, for editing and publishing (the already-converged Phase 1-3 path):

11. `getAuthoringV2(itemId)` (useCanonicalEditor.ts:67) → `get_authoring_v2_core` → `load_current_authoring`: revision > 0 → read `authoring/revisions/<n>`; revision == 0 → read `authoring-ir-v2.shadow.json`. No authoring_pipeline involvement.
12. `applyAuthoringV2Patches` (useCanonicalEditor.ts:107) → `apply_authoring_v2_patches_core` → apply patches → `mark_user_audit` → `refresh_quality_report` (reads document-ir-v2.shadow.json for source coverage) → `validate_authoring` → `append_revision`. From revision 1 onward the exam is genuinely V1-free on disk.
13. Publish: `publishItem` → `getAuthoringV2` → `exportAuthoringV2` (publishClient.ts:48-50) → `export_authoring_v2_core`: quality.state must be "ready", then `validate_authoring_v2_publish_readiness` (which requires source-review.json resolved — a DocumentIRV1-derived artifact), then `compile_reading_source_v2` → files → `exportNasPackageV2` → `publish_nas_package_v2_core`, which is entirely ReadingExamSourceV2 and has no V1 dependency.

Net: the ONLY thing V1 still decides is question structure at step 6, but that decision is laundered into every downstream V2 artifact at step 8. DocumentIRV2 is currently a decoration layer, not a structure source.

### 关键签名
- `make_dynamic_split_candidates` — `pub(crate) fn make_dynamic_split_candidates(     job_id: &str,     job: &ImportJob,     doc: Option<&Value>, ) -> Value`
  - V1 entry point #1. Returns a `SplitCandidatesV1{...}.to_value()` (constructed at line 4,721, serialized at 4,744). Falls back to a hardcoded demo blob `split_candidates(job_id)` (line 255) when `dynamic_document_blocks(doc)` is empty — silently produces a fake 'Detective Stories' exam. 6 production call sites.
- `make_dynamic_authoring_ir` — `pub(crate) fn make_dynamic_authoring_ir(     job: &ImportJob,     split: &Value,     doc: Option<&Value>, ) -> Value`
  - V1 entry point #2. Returns `ReadingAuthoringIrV1{...}.to_value()` (constructed at 8,809, serialized at 8,861), schemaVersion "ReadingAuthoringIRV1". Requires BOTH the split value and the document; passage HTML is the concatenation of `dynamic_block_html` over the split's passage range.
- `merge_answer_source_candidates` — `pub(crate) fn merge_answer_source_candidates(split: &mut Value, answer_candidates: Vec<Value>)`
  - Mutates split in place to fold in answer-key sidecar files. Always paired with make_dynamic_split_candidates.
- `dynamic_document_blocks` — `pub(crate) fn dynamic_document_blocks(doc: Option<&Value>) -> Vec<Value>`
  - Generic DocumentIRV1 flattener: pages[].blocks[] → sorted reading order, injecting private `_epic8PageWidth/_epic8PageHeight/_epic8PageRotation/_epic8OriginalOrder` keys. Used well outside the V1 pipeline: source_review.rs:79+103, llm_commands.rs:348, auto_pipeline.rs:1373. This one cannot simply be frozen.
- `dynamic_block_text` — `pub(crate) fn dynamic_block_text(block: &Value) -> String`
  - Prefers `text`, falls back to strip_html(`html`). Used by source_review.rs, llm_commands.rs:352, auto_pipeline.rs:98/1375.
- `dynamic_block_id` — `pub(crate) fn dynamic_block_id(block: &Value) -> String`
  - 
- `dynamic_block_role` — `pub(crate) fn dynamic_block_role(block: &Value) -> &str`
  - 
- `collapse_whitespace` — `pub(crate) fn collapse_whitespace(input: &str) -> String`
  - Plan §17.2 says to delete the new chain's dependency on this. Still used by parser.rs at lines 275, 1326, 1540, 1632 — including inside the DOCX table/paragraph builders, not only the V1 chain.
- `has_dynamic_ielts_question_instruction_evidence` — `pub(crate) fn has_dynamic_ielts_question_instruction_evidence(text: &str) -> bool`
  - Inverted dependency: parser.rs:69 (`role_hint_for_text`) calls INTO authoring_pipeline. The parser cannot be separated from the V1 recognizer until this moves.
- `parse_dynamic_answer_text` — `pub(crate) fn parse_dynamic_answer_text(text: &str) -> serde_json::Map<String, Value>`
  - Answer-key text → {displayNumber: answer}. Used by auto_pipeline.rs:98 inside `parse_answer_source_candidates`, which serves answer-key sidecar SOURCE FILES, not the main question document. A V2-direct path still needs this capability.
- `dynamic_completion_foreign_slots` — `pub(crate) fn dynamic_completion_foreign_slots(     prompt: &str,     number: u32,     range_start: u32,     range_end: u32, ) -> Vec<u32>`
  - Sole consumer is authoring_review.rs:503 (V1 review defect COMPLETION_PROMPT_FOREIGN_SLOTS). Dies with V1 review.
- `dynamic_interaction_for_kind` — `pub(crate) fn dynamic_interaction_for_kind(kind: &str) -> Value`
  - Sole consumer llm_suggestions.rs:99 (V1 patch synthesis).

| wrong | §17.2 target interface: `pub async fn ingest_source_to_document_v2(source: &StoredSourceAsset, options: IngestOptions) -> Result<DocumentIRV2>` | None of `ingest_source_to_document_v2`, `build_document_ir_v2`, `IngestOptions`, `StoredSourceAsset` exists anywhere in src-tauri (grep over all .rs returns zero hits). The actual V2 producers are synchronous and V1-shaped: `write_pdf_facts_shadow_with_v1(job, source, input_path, output_path, v1_document) -> CommandResult<Value>` and its DOCX twin. There is no typed `DocumentIRV2` return anywhere — `serde_json::from_value::<DocumentIRV2>` is used only as a validation round-trip (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:497-500). | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_facts_shadow.rs:474; F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/docx_facts_shadow.rs:37; grep for the four plan symbols returns nothing` |
| partly-wrong | §17.2 «新 PDF/DOCX 主线调用 build_document_ir_v2()» | The main line calls `parser::parse_source_document`, which for PDF uses pdfium-render (pdf_geometry.rs:177) and produces DocumentIRV1. DocumentIRV2 is produced by a SECOND, independent scan using pdf_extract + lopdf (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:11-13, 843-846). Every PDF is therefore parsed twice by two different engines, and only the V1 result drives structure. On the auto-import path the V2 shadow is PDF-only — DOCX gets no V2 shadow at all unless the legacy `parse_document` command is used. | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/auto_pipeline.rs:1449 vs 1496; F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/authoring_commands.rs:195-209` |

But `markdownish_to_html_pub` IS still called on the good path (pdf_geometry.rs:2318), and `collapse_whitespace` is used inside the DOCX paragraph/table builders (parser.rs:1540, 1632), not only by the V1 recognizer. | `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/pdf_geometry.rs:2301-2327; F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/parser.rs:524-534, 1540, 1632` |
