### 现有测试方式
Three layers, all colocated `#[cfg(test)]` — there is no `src-tauri/tests/` integration directory.

1. Unit / behavior corpus — `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/authoring_pipeline.rs`, `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` at line 8,864 to EOF (4,985 lines, 99 `#[test]`). Helpers to copy verbatim: `test_job()` (8,871), `layout_block(...)` (8,888), `two_column_option_table(rows: &[(&str,&str)])` (8,907), `layout_block_on_page(...)` (8,934). Every test builds `json!({"schemaVersion":"DocumentIRV1","pages":[{"pageIndex":N,"width":..,"height":..,"blocks":[...]}],"assets":[]})` then asserts on `make_dynamic_split_candidates` and/or `make_dynamic_authoring_ir`. Representative names showing exactly what the V2-direct recognizer must reproduce: `detect_dynamic_group_kind_covers_form_map_and_letter_matching_variants` (8,963), `tea_style_roman_table_populates_all_heading_options` (8,997), `heading_matching_uses_roman_bank_not_paragraph_letters` (9,031), `declared_a_j_bank_keeps_the_j_option_across_detection_and_filtering` (9,213), `explicit_letter_bank_turns_completion_interactions_into_selectable_matching` (9,254), `completion_without_an_explicit_letter_bank_remains_free_text` (9,336), `bare_question_number_with_split_option_lines_recovers_prompt_and_abc` (9,444), `matching_bank_closes_across_interleaved_next_heading_by_geometry` (9,625), `matching_bank_recovers_cross_column_final_label_after_next_heading` (9,669), `rotated_text_matrix_recovers_number_without_visual_space` (9,729). Copy the INPUT FIXTURES (they are real IELTS layouts distilled to block JSON), not the assertions — the assertions are shaped around SplitCandidatesV1/ReadingAuthoringIrV1 field names.

2. Command-level integration — `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/lib.rs`, `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` at line 1,480 (136 `#[test]`). Roughly 60 of them call `make_dynamic_split_candidates` + `make_dynamic_authoring_ir` against a temp app root; imports at lib.rs:1,483-1,494 pull in the authoring_pipeline entry points, `run_auto_pipeline_core_with_gateway`, `cleanup_transient_job_artifacts` and the authoring_commands `_core` fns. Load-bearing examples: `run_auto_pipeline_core(&root, &job.job_id, None)` at 4,327 / 4,464 / 4,683 / 4,869 / 4,932 / 9,864; `run_auto_pipeline_core_with_gateway(...)` with a stub LLM closure at 4,403 / 4,521 / 4,772 / 6,030 / 6,268 / 9,737 / 9,974 / 10,134; the draft-guard pair at 6,389-6,410 (`run_rule_split_core(...).unwrap_err()` then with allowOverwrite); `run_cloud_review_core_with_gateway` at 10,812 with a comment about the read-compute-write window. These will break loudest and are the honest signal that a V2-direct path changed observable behavior.

3. Real-PDF acceptance (the actual gate) — `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/src-tauri/src/ielts_grammar/real_pdf_acceptance.rs`, declared `#[cfg(test)] mod real_pdf_acceptance;` at `ielts_grammar/mod.rs:23-24`. Spec: `F:/workspace/PDF2Test/fixtures/golden/phase4-eight-pdf-acceptance.json` — 8 fixtureIds (chili-peppers, conformity, fishbourne-roman-palace, listening-to-the-ocean, organisational-design, petri-dish, sleep-study, western-celebrity), 11 requiredProofs (including `real_pdf_physical_document_ir_v2`, `physical_to_authoring_v2_to_quality`, `organisational_shared_prompt_two_slots`, `petri_single_list_of_people_bank`, `western_questions_before_passage`, `ocean_q9_q13_inline_a_d`, `answer_raster_excluded_from_passage`), and a `policy` block asserting `v1Authoritative: true`, `v2ShadowOnly: true`, `productionExposure: false`, `falseReadyForbidden: true`. Manifest at `fixtures/golden/manifest.json`; PDFs under `fixtures/golden/private-real/`. Key tests: `phase4_eight_real_pdfs_reach_physical_authoring_quality_truth` (1,945) and `phase5_real_pdf_edit_and_v2_export_round_trip` (2,016) — together these run the full chain `parse_source_document` → `write_pdf_facts_shadow` → `make_dynamic_split_candidates` → `make_dynamic_authoring_ir` → `build_authoring_v2_shadow` → `apply_authoring_v2_patches_core` → `export_authoring_v2_core`. Reports land at `tmp/phase4-real-pdf-acceptance/report.json` and `tmp/phase5-real-pdf-acceptance/report.json` (constants at lines 16-19). Two `#[ignore]`d private-PDF probes exist for targeted repair: `chili_real_pdf_reaches_all_declared_acceptance_truth` (1,893) and `petri_real_pdf_reaches_all_declared_acceptance_truth` (1,920) — copy that `#[ignore]` pattern for a per-fixture V2-direct probe. Policy-shaped unit tests in the same file that will need updating: `quality_acceptance_policy_rejects_unexplained_blockers_and_requires_live_ocr_evidence` (1,820), `quality_acceptance_policy_limits_runtime_exception_to_organisational_unresolved_set` (1,860), `compiler_probe_chain_requires_honest_failure_semantics` (1,788).

Coverage asymmetry worth knowing: `parser.rs` has only 4 `#[test]` across two blocks (2,733 and 2,808) despite being the sole DocumentIRV1 producer, and `pdf_geometry.rs`'s tests (boundary at 2,883) are the only place the pdfium path is exercised — including the make_dynamic_* calls at 3,156/3,162, which are tests, not production. Per the assignment I read at interface level and did not run cargo build or cargo test, so these are static observations.

### 意外发现
- The headline finding: DocumentIRV2 is decoration, not structure. `ielts_grammar::build_authoring_v2_shadow` (mod.rs:76) takes `v1_authoring: &Value` and `split: &Value` as REQUIRED parameters and iterates `split["questionGroupCandidates"]` (line 131) to emit taskGroups; `physical_shadow` is `Option<&Value>` and feeds only `physical_lines` used as a geometry fallback (105-115). Every V2 artifact the new workspace shows is therefore a V1 decision wearing a V2 schema. 'Freeze and isolate' per §17.6 is necessary but nowhere near sufficient — someone has to write a DocumentIRV2 structure recognizer that does not exist in any form today.
- Every PDF is parsed twice by two different engines. DocumentIRV1 comes from pdfium-render (pdf_geometry.rs, `use pdfium_render::prelude::*`); DocumentIRV2 comes from pdf_extract + lopdf (pdf_facts_shadow.rs, `use pdf_extract::{ColorSpace, Document, MediaBox, OutputDev, ...}`). Plan §17.3 mentions unifying them in one line; in practice these are two independent extraction stacks with independent bugs, and the V2 one is not the one with real glyph geometry from pdfium.
- V1 is still a live publish gate on the pure-V2 path. `probe_v1_compatibility` (quality.rs:952) compiles V2 authoring down to ReadingExamSourceV1; failure becomes `severity: "blocking"` (913) → hardFailures (3,635) → `state = "blocked"` (190) → `export_authoring_v2_core` returns `authoring_v2_export_blocked:quality_state=blocked` (481-487). You cannot delete reading_source.rs's V1 compiler as §20.2 implies without first deciding what happens to this probe.
- authoring_pipeline.rs has no typed public API at all. Zero `pub` items; 9 `pub(crate) fn`; the two entry points return bare `serde_json::Value`. The V1 structs exist (SplitCandidatesV1, ReadingAuthoringIrV1) but are serialized through private `to_value()` methods and never appear in a signature — so the compiler will not help you find breakage when the artifact shape changes. And 4,985 of its 13,848 lines (36%) are its own test module.
- All three V2 feature flags are hardcoded `-> true` with no env override: `document_ir_v2_shadow_enabled` (environment.rs:219), `authoring_v2_shadow_enabled` (223), `quality_gate_v2_enabled` (227). There is nothing to stage a V2-direct rollout behind and no way to roll back without a recompile — unusual given that every other knob in environment.rs reads an EPIC8_* env var.
- Opening an old job needs zero V1 code. `get_authoring_v2` → `build_editor_session` → `load_current_authoring` (authoring_v2_commands.rs:725) reads `authoring/revisions/<n>` when revision > 0, else `authoring-ir-v2.shadow.json`, and never touches authoring_pipeline. §17.6's stated justification for keeping the V1 pipeline ('旧任务打开') is the one use case that is already free of it.
- V1 artifacts are already ephemeral, but the wrong one is kept. `minimize_process_artifacts_after_authoring` (cleanup.rs:160) deletes document-ir.json and split-candidates.json immediately after authoring while keeping authoring-ir.json — and `write_authoring_project` (cleanup.rs:44) HARD-reads that kept file with `read_json(...)?`. So the recognizer's inputs are thrown away and only its output is load-bearing, which also means re-running run_rule_split on an old job silently re-parses from uploads/ with whatever the current code does.
- The re-run guard is keyed on the V1 artifact, and the frontend is already inconsistent about it. `if dir.join("authoring-ir.json").exists() && !allow_overwrite` (auto_pipeline.rs:1402); ExamWorkspacePage.tsx:115 passes allowOverwrite:true but :173 and useImportFiles.ts:149 do not. A V2-direct path that stops writing authoring-ir.json inherits a guard that never fires — i.e. silent clobbering of user edits.
- Every `update_job` appends a library revision row carrying the full V1 IR. `job_store::save_job` (33-44) unconditionally calls `upsert_reading_job`, which reads authoring-ir.json as payload and writes `library_items` + a new revision with `schema_version: "ReadingAuthoringIRV1"` hardcoded (library_commands.rs:106). One import produces several such rows. The verified fact library_items.id == job.job_id is confirmed here: id, linked_ingest_job_id and created_from_job_id are all exam.id == job.job_id.
- `make_dynamic_split_candidates` silently fabricates a demo exam. When `dynamic_document_blocks(doc)` is empty it returns the hardcoded `split_candidates(job_id)` blob (line 255) — a fake 'The Rise and Fall of Detective Stories' passage with invented TRUE/FALSE/NOT GIVEN answers. Any V2-direct migration comparing old jobs against V1 output will hit these phantom exams and should treat them as a data-quality finding, not a regression.
- PassageDraftV1 destroys per-block geometry. `make_dynamic_authoring_ir` collapses the entire passage into ONE html_blocks entry with block_id "passage-main" (authoring_pipeline.rs:8,836-8,846) built by concatenating `dynamic_block_html` with newlines. Anything downstream that wants per-paragraph source anchors has to recover them from `source_block_ids`, which is why `group_lines` (ielts_grammar/mod.rs:555) has a three-tier fallback (sectionEvidence → v1_lines by blockId → all fallback_lines).
- parser.rs calls INTO authoring_pipeline. `role_hint_for_text` (parser.rs:57-74) uses `has_dynamic_ielts_question_instruction_evidence` at line 69. The parser cannot be separated from the V1 recognizer until that predicate moves, so §17.2 ('keep parser, freeze pipeline') is not independently actionable in the stated order.
- `merge_v1_diagram_question_region_assets` can hard-fail V2 production from V1 state: if a V1 asset carrying `diagramQuestionRegion` points at a file that no longer exists, it returns Err `diagram_question_region_source_asset_missing` (pdf_facts_shadow.rs:543), aborting the whole DocumentIRV2 write. Since cleanup removes cache/ and preview/, this is a real staleness hazard on re-parse.
- On the auto-import path, DOCX never gets a DocumentIRV2 shadow at all. The shadow block at auto_pipeline.rs:1485-1520 filters `source.file_type == "pdf"`; only the legacy `parse_document` command handles DOCX (authoring_commands.rs:203). So today DOCX imports are 100% V1 with no V2 physical evidence, and the quality report's source-coverage scoring runs with `physical_available: false`.
- Two production call sites are easy to mistake for tests and vice versa. `lib.rs:474-479` (`generate_reading_source_from_path`) is real non-test code — a CLI fixture generator sitting ~1,000 lines above the `#[cfg(test)]` boundary at 1,480 — while `pdf_geometry.rs:3,156-3,162` looks like production but is past that file's `#[cfg(test)]` boundary at 2,883. Grepping for callers without checking the boundaries gives the wrong answer in both directions.
